\epsilon=\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}=\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2+C_{T}d_{\mathcal{Y}})}
in Lemma
E.2
and replacing
p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})
with
|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}\frac{|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}},

and

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\log\mathcal{M}\cdot\int_{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}\frac{|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}.

For
t\in[\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}},8\mathcal{M}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}]
, we have
m_{t}+\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})=\mathcal{O}(1)
for sufficiently large
\mathcal{M}
. When
m_{t}<\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})=\mathcal{O}(1)
and
\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})
, then
1-\mathcal{O}(1)\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\leq\|\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq 1
. Since
t\in[\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}},8\mathcal{M}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}]
,
\mathcal{O}(1)\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\leq a
holds for sufficiently large
\mathcal{M}
.
Therefore, by Lemma
A.2
, it holds that

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\int_{m_{t}<\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}\int_{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}\frac{|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{m_{t}<\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}\int_{1-a<\|\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq 1}\frac{|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{1-a<\|\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq 1}|p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\lesssim\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+4+C_{T}d_{\mathcal{Y}})}=\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+4)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\int_{m_{t}<\|\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+\sigma_{t}\cdot\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\geq\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)}\}}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})}{\sigma_{t}}-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

(20)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{4\beta+2}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\cdot\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+4)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}+\frac{\mathcal{M}^{4\beta+2}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\cdot\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}},

where we used
m_{t}\geq T
.
Combining (
17
) and (
20
), we finally obtain (III)
\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}
.
The proof is complete.
∎

Next, we approximate
\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
via ReLU neural networks on
[6\mathcal{M}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}},1]
. Let
t_{0}=2\mathcal{M}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}
, by the Markov property, we have

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t,t_{0}}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1},

where

m_{t,t_{0}}:=\frac{1-t}{1-t_{0}},\penalty\ \sigma_{t,t_{0}}:=\sqrt{1-\frac{(1-t)^{2}}{(1-t_{0})^{2}}}.

Since
t_{0}=2\mathcal{M}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}
, for sufficiently large
\mathcal{M}
, we know that
1/2\leq 1-t_{0}<1
, therefore, we have

2m_{t}\geq m_{t,t_{0}}\geq m_{t}

and

\sigma_{t}^{2}\geq\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{2}=\sigma_{t}^{2}-\frac{(1-t)^{2}}{(1-t_{0})^{2}}\sigma_{t_{0}}^{2}\geq\sigma_{t}^{2}-\sigma_{t_{0}}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{3}\sigma_{t}^{2},

where we used

\frac{\sigma_{t_{0}}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}=\frac{t_{0}(2-t_{0})}{t(2-t)}\leq\frac{2t_{0}}{t}\leq\frac{4\mathcal{M}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}}{6\mathcal{M}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}}=\frac{2}{3}.

We first approximate
p_{t_{0}}
and give the following lemma.

Lemma A.7
.

Let
\mathcal{M}\gg 1,C_{T}>0
,
\overline{C}_{T}=1-\widetilde{C}_{T}/2>0
. There exists a constant
C_{7}>0
and a function
p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}
such that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\left|p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}},

where
p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})
has the following form:

\displaystyle p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle=t_{0}^{-\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in[\mathcal{M}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}},\mathbf{n}\in[\mathcal{M}]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}}}\sum_{\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}\|_{1}<k_{0}}\widetilde{c}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\cdot p_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{y}}{2(1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}+\frac{1}{2},\frac{\mathbf{x}+1}{2}\right)\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\}},

with
k_{0}=\lfloor\frac{3\beta+2+C_{T}d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}{\overline{C}_{T}}\rfloor+1
and
\widetilde{c}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}=\mathcal{O}(\log^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{M})
.

Proof.

Let
k_{0}\geq 1
,
0\leq\ell\leq k_{0}
. According to Lemma
E.3
, for any
\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
,
\mathbf{x}\in[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}}
, we have

\|\partial_{y_{i_{1}}\cdots y_{i_{k_{0}-\ell}}}\partial_{x_{j_{1}},\cdots x_{j_{\ell}}}p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t_{0}}^{k_{0}-\ell}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t_{0}}^{k_{0}}}\lesssim t_{0}^{-\frac{k_{0}}{2}}.

Thus
t_{0}^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\in\mathcal{H}^{k_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}},C_{k_{0}})
with some constant
C_{k_{0}}>0
. By replacing
p_{t_{0}}
with
p_{t_{0}}^{2}
in Lemma
E.4
, we can claim that there exists a constant
C_{7}
such that

\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}p_{t_{0}}^{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\leq\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t_{0}}+C_{7}\sigma_{t_{0}}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}p_{t_{0}}^{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}.

Similar to the proof of Lemma
A.1
, we let

f(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})=t_{0}^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}p_{t_{0}}\left((1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})(2\mathbf{y}-1)\mid 2\mathbf{x}-1\right),\penalty\ \mathbf{y}\in[0,1]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}},\mathbf{x}\in[0,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}}.

Then,
\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}([0,1]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[0,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}})}\lesssim 2^{k_{0}}(1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})^{k_{0}}=\mathcal{O}(\log^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{M})
. Thus, there exists a function
p_{\mathcal{M},0}
such that

\left|p_{\mathcal{M},0}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-t_{0}^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right|\lesssim\mathcal{M}^{-k_{0}}\log^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{M},\penalty\ \|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}},\penalty\ \mathbf{x}\in[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}}

where

\displaystyle p_{\mathcal{M},0}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in[\mathcal{M}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}},\mathbf{n}\in[\mathcal{M}]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}}}\sum_{\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}\|_{1}<k_{0}}\widetilde{c}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\cdot p_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{y}}{2(1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}+\frac{1}{2},\frac{\mathbf{x}+1}{2}\right)\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\}}

with
\widetilde{c}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}=\mathcal{O}(\log^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{M})
.

Next, we define
p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x}):=t_{0}^{-\frac{k_{0}}{2}}p_{\mathcal{M},0}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})
, then we have

\displaystyle\left|p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{M}^{-k_{0}}t_{0}^{-\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\log^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{M}^{-k_{0}(1-\widetilde{C}_{T}/2)}\log^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{M}^{-k_{0}\overline{C}_{T}}\log^{\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{M}.

Let
k_{0}=\lfloor\frac{3\beta+2+C_{T}d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}{\overline{C}_{T}}\rfloor+1\geq\frac{3\beta+2+C_{T}d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}{\overline{C}_{T}}
, then we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}\left|p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{-(6\beta+4)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{k_{0}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{M}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}

for sufficient large
\mathcal{M}
. Subsequently,
p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}
satisfies that

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\left|p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle=\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}\left|p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}+\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}p_{t_{0}}^{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma A.8
.

Let
\mathcal{M}\gg 1
. There exists a ReLU neural network
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
with

L=\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M}),M=\mathcal{O}(\mathcal{M}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{7}\mathcal{M}),J=\mathcal{O}({\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{9}\mathcal{M}),\kappa=\exp\left(\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M})\right)

that satisfies

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}},\penalty\ \penalty\ t\in[6{\mathcal{M}}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}},1-\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}}].

Moreover, we can take
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}
satisfying
\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\cdot,\mathbf{x})\|_{\infty}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t}}
.

Proof.

The proof is similar to the proof of Lemma
A.6
.
We also decompose the approximation error into three terms.

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}.}_{(\mathrm{I})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq\epsilon_{4}\}}\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}}_{(\mathrm{II})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})>\epsilon_{4}\}}\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}}_{(\mathrm{III})}.

According to Lemma
E.4
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that for any
\epsilon_{3}>0
,

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}}_{(\mathrm{I})}

(21)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{3}}{\sigma_{t}}+\sigma_{t}\epsilon_{3}\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\cdot,\mathbf{x})\|_{\infty}^{2}.

Since
\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}}{\sigma_{t}}
in
\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}
due to Lemma
E.3
,
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}
can be taken so that
\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\cdot,\mathbf{x})\|_{\infty}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}}{\sigma_{t}}
. Therefore (
21
)
\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{3}\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}{\sigma_{t}}\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{3}\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}
. Taking
\epsilon_{3}=\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)}
, then (
21
)
\lesssim\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{-(2\beta+1)}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}
, which is smaller than
\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}
. Moreover, taking
\epsilon_{4}=\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)}
and by Lemma
E.4
, we can bound the error

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq\epsilon_{4}\}}\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}}_{(\mathrm{II})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{4}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot(\log\epsilon^{-1}_{3})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2}{2}}+\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\cdot,\mathbf{x})\|_{\infty}^{2}\epsilon_{3}\cdot(\log\epsilon^{-1}_{4})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{-(2\beta+1)}\log^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2}{2}}\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}},

which is smaller than
\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}
for sufficiently large
\mathcal{M}
. Thus, we can only focus on bounding the term (III).

We first take
C_{7}\geq C\sqrt{2\beta+1}
in Lemma
A.7
and
C_{0}=1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})
.
Then, we replace
p_{0}
,
m_{t}
,
\sigma_{t}
with
p_{t_{0}}
,
m_{t,t_{0}}
and
\sigma_{t,t_{0}}
, and replace
\frac{y_{1}+1}{2}-\frac{m}{\mathcal{M}}
with

\frac{y_{1}}{2(1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{m}{\mathcal{M}}
in
f(t,y,m,\alpha,l)
. We also replace
C_{\beta}
,
C_{a}
and
c_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}
with
k_{0}
,
1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}
and
t_{0}^{-\frac{k_{0}}{2}}\widetilde{c}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n},\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\gamma}}
.
We still use the notations
g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})
,
\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})
and
\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})
, where

g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t,t_{0}}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y},

\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\}}\cdot\frac{\mathbf{y}-m_{t,t_{0}}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+1}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t,t_{0}}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1},

\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x}):=\max\left\{\min\left\{\frac{\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})}{g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\vee{\mathcal{M}}^{-(2\beta+1)}},\frac{C_{4}}{2}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\right\},-\frac{C_{4}}{2}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}\right\}.

Applying the argument of Lemma
A.6
, we can construct a ReLU neural network
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}
with network parameters

L=\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M}),M=\mathcal{O}({\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{7}\mathcal{M}),J=\mathcal{O}({\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{9}\mathcal{M}),\kappa=\exp\left(\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M})\right)

that satisfies

\left\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\frac{\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}\right\|_{\infty}\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{-\beta},\penalty\ \|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1+C_{7}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}},

and

\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\cdot,\mathbf{x})\|_{\infty}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{(2\beta+1)\log\mathcal{M}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\geq{\mathcal{M}}^{-(2\beta+1)}\}}\left\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\frac{\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{\mathcal{Y}}{\mathcal{M}}^{-2\beta}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{-2\beta}.

Next, we consider bound the term

\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{(2\beta+1)\log\mathcal{M}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\geq{\mathcal{M}}^{-(2\beta+1)}\}}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}.

For
1\leq i\leq d_{\mathcal{Y}}
, using
\left|[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}\right|\leq\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\leq\frac{C_{4}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t}}
and
\frac{\left|[\mathbf{h}\prime(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})]_{i}\right|}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}\leq\frac{C_{4}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{2\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}\leq\frac{C_{4}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t}}
, by the property of clipping function, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\left|\frac{[\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})]_{i}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}-[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{[\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})]_{i}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}(g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\vee\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)})}-[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{[\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})]_{i}-\sigma_{t,t_{0}}(g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\vee\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)})\cdot[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}(g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\vee\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}\left(p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})-g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\vee\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)}\right)}{g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\vee\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)}}\right|+\left|\frac{[\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})]_{i}-\sigma_{t,t_{0}}[\nabla p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}(g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\vee\mathcal{M}^{-(2\beta+1)})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{2\beta+1}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t}}\left|p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})-g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\right|+\frac{\mathcal{M}^{2\beta+1}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}\left|\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\sigma_{t,t_{0}}[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{2\beta+1}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t}}\left|p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})-g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})\right|+\frac{\mathcal{M}^{2\beta+1}}{\sigma_{t}}\left|\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\sigma_{t,t_{0}}[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{i}\right|.

Therefore, we obtain

\left\|\frac{\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})}{\sigma_{t}}-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right\|\lesssim\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{2\beta+1}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sigma_{t}}\left(|p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})-g_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})|+\|\mathbf{h}_{1}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\sigma_{t,t_{0}}\nabla p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{(2\beta+1)\log\mathcal{M}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\geq{\mathcal{M}}^{-(2\beta+1)}\}}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{h}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}}-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{4\beta+2}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}m_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{(2\beta+1)\log\mathcal{M}}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{|p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t,t_{0}}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t,t_{0}}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}|p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})|^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t,t_{0}}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t,t_{0}}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\Bigg)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{4\beta+2}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}m_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\left|p_{t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})-p_{\mathcal{M},t_{0}}(\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{x})\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{4\beta+2}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}m_{t,t_{0}}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\cdot{\mathcal{M}}^{-(6\beta+2)}T^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\lesssim\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}},

where we used
m_{t,t_{0}}\gtrsim m_{t}
and
m_{t}\geq T
.
Therefore, we finally obtain

(\mathrm{III})\lesssim\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}},

which implies that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Combining Lemma
A.6
and Lemma
A.8
, we immediately obtain Lemma
3.1
.

Proof of Lemma
3.1
.

According to Lemma
A.6
and Lemma
A.8
, there exist two ReLU neural networks
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(1)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})
and
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})
that approximate the conditional score function
\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
on
[{\mathcal{M}}^{-C_{T}},8{\mathcal{M}}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}]
and
[6{\mathcal{M}}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}},1-\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}}]
, respectively.
Therefore, setting
t_{1}={\mathcal{M}}^{-C_{T}}
,
t_{2}=6{\mathcal{M}}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}
,
s_{1}=8{\mathcal{M}}^{-\widetilde{C}_{T}}
and
s_{2}=1-\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}}
in Lemma
E.12
, we can construct a ReLU neural network

\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x}):=\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(1)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})+\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})

with network parameters

L=\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M}),M=\mathcal{O}(\mathcal{M}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{7}\mathcal{M}),J=\mathcal{O}({\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{9}\mathcal{M}),\kappa=\exp\left(\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M})\right)

that approximates
\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
with the approximation error

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1}^{2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(1)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}^{2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{score}}^{(2)}(t,\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1}^{2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}+\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}^{2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{M}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}.

Here, we use

0\leq\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1},\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}\leq 1
and
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1}+\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}=1
. The proof is complete.
∎

Appendix B
Statistical Error

In this section, we bound the statistical error and prove Lemma
3.2
. Then, combining Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, we prove Theorem
3.3
. We begin by providing an upper bound for
\ell_{\mathbf{b}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})
.

Upper bound for
\ell_{\mathbf{b}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})
.

By the definition of
\ell_{\mathbf{b}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})
, it holds that

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\mathbf{b}(t,m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sigma_{t}\mathbf{z},\mathbf{x})+\frac{\mathbf{z}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}\|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{t}.

Thus, we have

\ell_{\mathbf{b}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})\lesssim\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{1-{2\mathcal{M}}^{-C_{T}}}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{t(1-t)}\mathrm{d}t\lesssim\log^{2}\mathcal{M}

for sufficiently large
\mathcal{M}
.

Lipschitz continuity for
\ell_{\mathbf{b}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})
.

Note that we restrict ReLU neural networks into class
\mathcal{C}
. For any
\mathbf{b}_{1}
,
\mathbf{b}_{2}\in\mathcal{C}
, by the construction structure of
\mathbf{b}_{1}
,
\mathbf{b}_{2}
,
we have

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\left|\ell_{\mathbf{b}_{1}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})-\ell_{\mathbf{b}_{2}}((\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\|\left\|\mathbf{b}_{1}+\mathbf{b}_{2}+\frac{2\mathbf{z}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\mathbf{b}_{1}+\mathbf{b}_{2}+\frac{2\mathbf{z}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}+\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-2T}\left(\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\log\mathcal{M}\cdot\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{t(2-t)}\mathrm{d}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\log\mathcal{M}\cdot\left(\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}\cdot\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}([T,1-T]\times\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}\cdot\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}([T,1-T]\times[-\mathcal{O}(1)\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}},\mathcal{O}(1)\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}})}.

Covering number evaluation.

We denote the
\delta
-covering number of the neural network class
\mathcal{C}
as
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}
.
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}
is evaluated as follows:

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}_{\delta}

\displaystyle=\log\mathcal{N}\left(\mathcal{C},\delta,\|\cdot\|_{L^{\infty}([T,1-T]\times\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}})}\right)

\displaystyle=\log\mathcal{N}\left(\mathcal{C},\delta,\|\cdot\|_{L^{\infty}([T,1-T]\times[-\mathcal{O}(1)\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}},\mathcal{O}(1)\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim JL\log{\left(\frac{LM\kappa(1-{\mathcal{M}}^{-C_{T}}\vee\mathcal{O}(1)\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}{\delta}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{13}\mathcal{M}\left(\log^{4}\mathcal{M}+\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right),

where we used
L=\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M}),M=\mathcal{O}({\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{7}\mathcal{M}),J=\mathcal{O}(\mathcal{M}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{9}\mathcal{M}),\kappa=\exp\left(\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\mathcal{M})\right)
and
T=\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}}
. The details of this derivation
can be found in
(
Chen et al. 2022
, Lemma 5.3)
.

Proof of Lemma
3.2
.

Let
\ell(\mathbf{b},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})=\ell_{\mathbf{b}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})-\ell_{\mathbf{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})
and
\mathcal{D}^{\prime}=\{(\mathbf{x}_{1}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{0,1}^{F,\prime}),\cdots,(\mathbf{x}_{n}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{0,n}^{F,\prime})\}
be an independent copy of
\mathcal{D}
,
then for any
\mathbf{b}_{1}
,
\mathbf{b}_{2}\in\mathcal{C}
, there exists a constant
C_{8}>0
such that

\displaystyle|\ell(\mathbf{b}_{1},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})-\ell(\mathbf{b}_{2},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})|

\displaystyle=|\ell_{\mathbf{b}_{1}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})-\ell_{\mathbf{b}_{2}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})|

\displaystyle\leq C_{8}\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}\|\mathbf{b}_{1}-\mathbf{b}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}([T,1-T]\times\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}})}.

We first estimate
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})-2\overline{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})+\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{b}^{*})\right)
.
It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})-2\overline{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})+\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{b}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\prime}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F,\prime})-\ell_{\mathbf{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F,\prime})\right)\right]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\ell_{\mathbf{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right],

where

G(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}):=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\prime}}\left[\ell(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F,\prime})-2\ell(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})\right].

Let
\mathcal{C}_{\delta}
be the
\delta
-covering of
\mathcal{C}
with minimum cardinality
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}
, then for any
\mathbf{b}\in\mathcal{C}
, there exists a
\mathbf{b}_{\delta}\in\mathcal{C}_{\delta}
such that

\displaystyle|\ell(\mathbf{b},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})-\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F})|

\displaystyle\leq C_{8}\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}\|\mathbf{b}-\mathbf{b}_{\delta}\|_{L^{\infty}([T,1-T]\times\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\times[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}})}

\displaystyle\leq C_{8}\delta\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}.

Therefore, for
1\leq i\leq n
, we have

G(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\leq G(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})+3C_{8}\delta\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}.

Since
|\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})|\lesssim\log^{2}\mathcal{M}
, there exists a constant
C_{9}>0
such that

|\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})|\leq C_{9}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}
.
We have that
|\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\mathbb{E}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})|\leq 2C_{9}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}
. We denote
V^{2}=\mathrm{Var}[\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})]
, then we have

\displaystyle V^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq C_{8}^{2}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\ell_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\ell_{\mathbf{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right]

\displaystyle=C_{8}^{2}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})].

We obtain

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})]\geq\frac{V^{2}}{C_{8}^{2}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}.

By Bernstein’s inequality, for any
t>0
, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>t\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\prime}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F,\prime})\right]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\prime}}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F,\prime})\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>\frac{t}{2}+\frac{V^{2}}{2C_{8}^{2}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\exp\left(-\frac{nu^{2}}{2V^{2}+\frac{4uC_{9}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\frac{nt}{8\left(C_{8}^{2}+\frac{C_{9}}{3}\right)\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}\right),

where
u=\frac{t}{2}+\frac{V^{2}}{2C_{8}^{2}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}
, and we use
u\geq\frac{t}{2}
and
V^{2}\leq 2uC_{8}^{2}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}
.
Hence, for any
t>3C_{8}\delta\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>t\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\mathop{\mathrm{sup}}_{\mathbf{b}\in\mathcal{C}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\mathbf{b},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>t\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\mathop{\mathrm{max}}_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta}\in\mathcal{C}_{\delta}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>t-3C_{8}\delta\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{N}_{\delta}\mathop{\mathrm{max}}_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta}\in\mathcal{C}_{\delta}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\mathbf{b}_{\delta},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0.i}^{F})>t-3C_{8}\delta\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{N}_{\delta}\exp\left(-\frac{n(t-3C_{8}\delta\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M})}{8\left(C_{8}^{2}+\frac{C_{9}}{3}\right)\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}\right).

By setting
a=\left[3C_{8}\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}+8\left(C_{8}^{2}+\frac{C_{9}}{3}\right)\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\log\mathcal{N}_{\delta}\right]\delta
and
\delta=\frac{1}{n}
, then we obtain

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right]

(22)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G(\widehat{\mathbf{b}},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>t\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq a+\mathcal{N}_{\delta}\int_{a}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{n(t-3C_{8}\delta\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M})}{8\left(C_{8}^{2}+\frac{C_{9}}{3}\right)\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\left[3C_{8}\log^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{M}+8\left(C_{8}^{2}+\frac{C_{9}}{3}\right)\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\log\mathcal{N}_{\delta}\right]\delta+\frac{8\left(C_{8}^{2}+\frac{C_{9}}{3}\right)\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{15}\mathcal{M}\left(\log^{4}\mathcal{M}+\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)\delta+\frac{\log^{2}\mathcal{M}}{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\log^{15}\mathcal{M}\left(\log^{4}\mathcal{M}+\log n\right)}{n}.

Next, we estimate
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})-\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})\right)
.
Recall that

\overline{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})-\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right).

We decompose
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)
into
the following three terms:

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F}))}_{(A)}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F}))}_{(B)}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F}))}_{(C)},

where

\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}):=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(t,m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sigma_{t}\mathbf{z},\mathbf{x})+\frac{\mathbf{z}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\cdot\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}\leq r\}}\right),

and

\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}):=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{1}{1-t_{j}}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(t_{j},m_{t_{j}}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sigma_{t_{j}}\mathbf{z}_{j},\mathbf{x})+\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\sigma_{t_{j}}}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{z}_{j}\|_{\infty}\leq r\}}.

We estimate these three terms separately.
Firstly,

\displaystyle(A)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\cdot\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(t,m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F}+\sigma_{t}\mathbf{z},\mathbf{x}_{i})+\frac{\mathbf{z}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\cdot\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}>r\}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\log\mathcal{M}\cdot\mathbb{P}(\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}>r)+\mathbb{E}\left(\|\mathbf{z}\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}>r\}}\right)\right)\cdot\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{t(1-t)}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim\log\mathcal{M}\left(\log\mathcal{M}\cdot\mathbb{P}(\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}>r)+[\mathbb{E}(\|\mathbf{z}\|^{4})]^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\mathbb{P}({\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}>r})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\cdot\mathbb{P}({\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}>r})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\exp\left(-\frac{r^{2}}{4}\right).

Therefore, there exists a constant
C_{10}>0
such that

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F}))\right)\leq C_{10}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\exp\left(-\frac{r^{2}}{4}\right).

Next, we bound the second term. Let

h_{\mathbf{b}}(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\mathbf{z}):=\frac{1}{1-t}\left\|\mathbf{b}(t,m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sigma_{t}\mathbf{z},\mathbf{x})+\frac{\mathbf{z}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}\leq r\}},

then there exists a constant
C_{11}>0
such that

0\leq h_{\mathbf{b}}(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}\mathbf{z})\lesssim\frac{r^{2}+\log\mathcal{M}}{T}\leq C_{11}{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(r^{2}+\log\mathcal{M}):=E_{\mathcal{M}}(r).

For any
\delta_{1}>0
,
\mathbf{b}\in\mathcal{C}
, there exists a
\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}\in\mathcal{C}_{\delta_{1}}
and a constant
C_{12}>0
such that

\displaystyle|h_{\mathbf{b}}(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\mathbf{z})-h_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}}(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\mathbf{z})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta_{1}}{1-t}\left\|\mathbf{b}+\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}+\frac{2\mathbf{z}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}\leq r\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r+\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{T}\cdot\delta_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{12}{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(r+\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})\delta_{1}.

Then, for fixed pair
(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F}),1\leq i\leq n
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}h_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(t,\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z})\mathrm{d}t-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}h_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(t_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z}_{j})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathrm{sup}}_{\mathbf{b}\in\mathcal{C}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}h_{\mathbf{b}}(t,\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z})\mathrm{d}t-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}h_{\mathbf{b}}(t_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z}_{j})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathrm{max}}_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta}\in\mathcal{C}_{\delta_{1}}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}h_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}}(t,\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z})\mathrm{d}t-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}h_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}}(t_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z}_{j})\right)

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+2C_{12}{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(r+\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})\delta_{1}.

Let
b=2C_{12}{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(r+\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})\delta_{1}
. For
t>b
, by Hoeffding’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}h_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(t,\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z})\mathrm{d}t-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}h_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(t_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z}_{j})>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathop{\mathrm{max}}_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}\in\mathcal{C}_{\delta_{1}}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}h_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}}(t,\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z})\mathrm{d}t-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}h_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}}(t_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z}_{j})\right)>t-b\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{\delta_{1}}\mathop{\mathrm{max}}_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}\in\mathcal{C}_{\delta_{1}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{z}}h_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}}(t,\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z})\mathrm{d}t-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}h_{\mathbf{b}_{\delta_{1}}}(t_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F},\mathbf{z}_{j})>t-b\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{\delta_{1}}\exp{\left(-\frac{2m(t-b)^{2}}{E_{\mathcal{M}}^{2}(r)}\right)}.

Therefore, by taking expectation over

\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}
, for any

c_{0}>0
, we deduce that
(B)
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})>t\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq b+c_{0}+\mathcal{N}_{\delta_{1}}\int_{c_{0}}^{+\infty}\exp{\left(-\frac{2mt^{2}}{E_{\mathcal{M}}^{2}(r)}\right)}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq b+c_{0}+\frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}\mathcal{N}_{\delta_{1}}\exp{\left(-\frac{2mc_{0}^{2}}{E_{\mathcal{M}}^{2}(r)}\right)}\frac{E_{\mathcal{M}}(r)}{\sqrt{2m}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle b+c_{0}+\frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}\mathcal{N}_{\delta_{1}}\exp{\left(-\frac{2mc_{0}^{2}}{E_{\mathcal{M}}^{2}(r)}\right)}\frac{E_{\mathcal{M}}(r)}{\sqrt{2m}}.

The last term can be expressed as

(C)=-\frac{1}{mn}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(t_{j},m_{t_{j}}\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F}+\sigma_{t_{j}}\mathbf{z}_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i})+\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\sigma_{t_{j}}}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{z}_{j}\|_{\infty}>r\}}\leq 0,

which implies

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}^{\mathrm{trunc}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)\right)\leq 0.

Combining the above inequalities, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)\right]\leq b_{0}+c_{0}+\frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}\mathcal{N}_{\delta_{1}}\exp{\left(-\frac{2mc_{0}^{2}}{E_{\mathcal{M}}^{2}(r)}\right)}\frac{E_{\mathcal{M}}(r)}{\sqrt{2m}},

where
b_{0}=C_{10}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}\exp\left(-\frac{r^{2}}{4}\right)+2C_{12}{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(r+\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})\delta_{1}
.
By setting
c_{0}=E_{\mathcal{M}}(r)\sqrt{\frac{\log{\mathcal{N}_{\delta_{1}}}}{2m}}
,
r=2\sqrt{\log{m}}
, and
\delta_{1}=\frac{1}{m}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\ell_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})-\widehat{\ell}_{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{y}_{0,i}^{F})\right)\right]

(23)

\displaystyle\leq b_{0}+E_{\mathcal{M}}(r)\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}_{1/m}}+1}{\sqrt{2m}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}\mathcal{M}+{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(\sqrt{\log m}+\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}})}{m}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(\log m+\log\mathcal{M})\cdot\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}{2}}\log^{\frac{13}{2}}\mathcal{M}(\log^{2}\mathcal{M}+\sqrt{\log m})}{\sqrt{m}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{C_{T}}(\log m+\log\mathcal{M})\cdot\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}{2}}\log^{\frac{13}{2}}\mathcal{M}(\log^{2}\mathcal{M}+\sqrt{\log m})}{\sqrt{m}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Combining Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, we can prove Theorem
3.3
.

Proof of Theorem
3.3
.

By choosing
\mathcal{M}=\lfloor n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\rfloor+1\lesssim n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}
in Lemma
3.1
, the approximation error can be bounded as

\displaystyle\inf_{\mathbf{b}\in\mathcal{C}}\left(\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{b})-\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{b}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{{\mathcal{M}}^{-2\beta}\log\mathcal{M}}{(1-t)\sigma_{t}^{2}}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim{\mathcal{M}}^{-2\beta}\log^{2}\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{2}n.

Substituting
\mathcal{M}=\lfloor n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\rfloor+1\lesssim n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}
,
m=n^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+8\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}
and
C_{T}=2\beta
into (
22
) and (
23
), we have

\eqref{eq: statsitical_error1}\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d^{*}+2\beta}}\log^{19}n,\penalty\ \penalty\ \eqref{eq: statsitical_error2}\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d^{*}+2\beta}}\log^{\frac{19}{2}}n,

which implies that the statistical error can be bounded as

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})-2\overline{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})+\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{b}^{*})\right)+2\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})-\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}(\widehat{\mathbf{b}})\right)\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{19}n.

Therefore, we finally obtain

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{t}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{x}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t,\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right)

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\left(\frac{1}{1-2T}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{1-t}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{y}_{t}^{F},\mathbf{x}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(t,\mathbf{y}_{t}^{F},\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}^{F}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{19}n.

The proof is complete.
∎

Appendix C
Bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))\right]

In this section, we bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
. We have the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))].

In the following two subsections, we bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
and
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
separately.

C.1
Bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
.

In this subsection, we bound the term
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

and prove Lemma
3.5
.

Proof of Lemma
3.5
.

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
can be decomposed into following two terms:

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))].

The first term
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
satisfies that

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B},p_{T})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}-\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|_{\infty}\leq B\}}\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|_{\infty}>B\}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|^{4}\cdot\mathbb{P}(\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|_{\infty}>B)\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

Since
\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\overset{d}{=}\mathbf{y}_{T}^{F}\overset{d}{=}(1-T)\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sqrt{T(2-T)}\mathbf{z}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|^{4}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|(1-T)\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sqrt{T(2-T)}\mathbf{z}\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left((1-T)^{2}+T(2-T)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(T^{2}+1-2T+2T-T^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

And we also have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|_{\infty}>B)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|(1-T)\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sqrt{T(2-T)}\mathbf{z}\right\|_{\infty}>B\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}>\frac{B-1}{\sqrt{T(2-T)}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2d_{\mathcal{Y}}\exp\left(-\frac{(B-1)^{2}}{2T(2-T)}\right).

Using
e^{-x}\leq\frac{1}{x}
for
x>0
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}(\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}\|_{\infty}>B)^{\frac{1}{4}}\lesssim\exp\left(-\frac{(B-1)^{2}}{4T(2-T)}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{T(2-T)}\lesssim\sqrt{T},

which implies that

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]\lesssim\sqrt{T}.

The second term
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
satisfies that

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}_{1-T}-\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}_{T}^{F}-\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|-T\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sqrt{T(2-T)}\mathbf{z}\right\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(T^{2}+T(2-T))^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{T},

which implies that

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]\lesssim\sqrt{T}.

Combining the above inequalities ,we finally obtain

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]\lesssim\sqrt{T}\lesssim n^{-\frac{\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}.

(24)

The proof is complete.
∎

C.2
Bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

In this subsection, we bound the term
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
. We summarize the SDEs we ’ve defined and introduce a new SDE as follows

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}+\frac{2}{t}\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\right]\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\frac{2}{t}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_{t},\quad\mathbf{y}_{0}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}),\ \mathbf{y}_{1}\sim p_{0}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\overline{\mathbf{y}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{\overline{\mathbf{y}}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}+\frac{2\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\overline{\mathbf{y}}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right]\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\frac{2}{t}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_{t},\quad\overline{\mathbf{y}}_{t_{0}}=\mathbf{y}_{t_{0}}=\mathbf{y}_{T},\ t\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}+\frac{2\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right]\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\frac{2}{t}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_{t},\quad\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{t_{0}}=\mathbf{y}_{0}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}),\ t\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})

where
T=t_{0}<t_{1}<\cdots<t_{K}=1-T
and
\frac{t_{i+1}}{t_{i}}\leq 2
for
0\leq i\leq K-1
.

Since
\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x})
and
p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x})
are supported on bounded region
[-B,B]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
, we obtain the inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\overline{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(\overline{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\widetilde{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}))],

where in the last line, we use the data processing inequality. Therefore, we only need to bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
and
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}))]
.

We bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}))]
firstly and gives the following lemma.

Lemma C.1
.

Suppose
\mathcal{M}=\lfloor n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\rfloor+1\lesssim n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}},T=\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}}
and
C_{T}=2\beta
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}))]\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}.

(25)

Proof of Lemma
C.1
.

Using Pinsker’ s inequality, we derive that

\displaystyle\mathrm{TV}\left(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}})\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}\left(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x})\left|\right.\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}})\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}\left(\int p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\mathbf{x})p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}\left|\right.\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}})\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\int\mathrm{KL}\left(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\mathbf{x})\left|\right.\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}})\right)p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}},

where the last inequality follows from the convexity of KL divergence. Note that
p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\mathbf{x})\sim\mathcal{N}(m_{1-T}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\sigma_{1-T}^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}})
and the KL divergence between two Gaussian distributions has the explicit form of

\mathrm{KL}(\rho_{1}|\rho_{2})=\frac{1}{2}\left((\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})^{T}\Sigma_{2}^{-1}(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})+\log\left(\frac{|\Sigma_{2}|}{|\Sigma_{1}|}\right)+\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{2}^{-1}\Sigma_{1})-d\right),

where
\rho_{1}\sim\mathcal{N}_{d}(\mu_{1},\Sigma_{1}),\penalty\ \rho_{1}\sim\mathcal{N}_{d}(\mu_{2},\Sigma_{2})
. We thus have

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}\left(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F},\mathbf{x})\left|\right.\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}})\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\left\|m_{1-T}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}\right\|^{2}-\log\sigma_{1-T}^{2d_{\mathcal{Y}}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}\sigma_{1-T}^{2}-d_{\mathcal{Y}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{2}-d_{\mathcal{Y}}\log(1-T^{2})+d_{\mathcal{Y}}((1-T^{2})-1)

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{2}.

Finally we get

\mathrm{TV}\left(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}})\right)\lesssim\sqrt{T^{2}\cdot\int p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}}\lesssim T\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}},

and the desired result follows.
∎

To bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
, we first introduce the following lemma.

Lemma C.2

(Proposition D.1 in
Oko et al. 2023
)
.

Let
\pi_{0}
be any probability distribution, and
\mathbf{z}=(\mathbf{z}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}
,
\mathbf{z}^{\prime}=(\mathbf{z}^{\prime}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}
be two different processes satisfying

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\mathbf{z}_{t}=\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})\mathrm{d}t+\sigma(t)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_{t},\penalty\ \mathbf{z}_{0}\sim\pi_{0},

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\mathbf{z}^{\prime}_{t}=\mathbf{b}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{z}^{\prime}_{t})\mathrm{d}t+\sigma(t)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_{t},\penalty\ \mathbf{z}^{\prime}_{0}\sim\pi_{0}.

We define the distributions of
\mathbf{z}_{t}
and
\mathbf{z}^{\prime}_{t}
as
\pi_{t}
and
\pi^{\prime}_{t}
, and the path measures of
\mathbf{z}
and
\mathbf{z}^{\prime}
as
\mathbb{P}
and
\mathbb{P}^{\prime}
, respectively. Suppose that the Novikov’s condition holds, i.e.,

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\frac{\|\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})-\mathbf{b}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}(t)}\mathrm{d}t\right)\right]<+\infty.

(26)

Then, the Radon-Nikodym derivative of
\mathbb{P}^{\prime}
with respect to
\mathbb{P}
is

\frac{\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}^{\prime}}{\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}}=\exp\left(-\int_{0}^{T}\frac{\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})-\mathbf{b}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})}{\sigma(t)}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}_{t}-\int_{0}^{T}\frac{\|\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})-\mathbf{b}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})\|^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}(t)}\mathrm{d}t\right),

and therefore we have

\mathrm{KL}(\pi_{T}|\pi_{T}^{\prime})\leq\mathrm{KL}(\mathbb{P}|\mathbb{P}^{\prime})=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\frac{\|\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})-\mathbf{b}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}(t)}\mathrm{d}t\right].

Moreover, if there exists a constant
C>0
such that for any
t\in[0,T]
it holds

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\frac{\|\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})-\mathbf{b}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}(t)}\right]\leq C,

then even if the Novikov’ s condition is not satisfied, we can still derive that

\mathrm{KL}(\pi_{T}|\pi_{T}^{\prime})\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\frac{\|\mathbf{b}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})-\mathbf{b}^{\prime}(t,\mathbf{z}_{t})\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}(t)}\mathrm{d}t\right].

Based on Lemma
C.2
, we can prove Theorem
3.4
.

Proof of Theorem
3.4
.

For any
t\in[T,1-T]
, we have
\mathbf{y}_{t}\sim p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
and
\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t,\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\mathcal{M}}}{\sqrt{1-t}}
. Moreover, we claim that
\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}]\lesssim\frac{1}{1-t}
. In fact, by Lemma
E.3
, it holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{1-t}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}-m_{1-t})_{+}}{\sigma_{1-t}}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma_{1-t}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|m_{1-t}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sigma_{1-t}\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}-m_{1-t})_{+}}{\sigma_{1-t}}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{1-t}}\cdot\left(\frac{(m_{1-t}+\sigma_{1-t}\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}-m_{1-t})_{+}}{\sigma_{1-t}}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-t^{2}}}\cdot\left(\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-t}}\cdot\left(\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}+1\right).

Taking the square and expectation, we derive the result. Therefore, for
0\leq i\leq K-1
,
t\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})
, it holds

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\frac{t}{2}\cdot\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}+2\left(\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t}{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{t^{2}}+\frac{1}{t_{i}^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}(1-t)}+\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{t_{i}^{2}(1-t_{i})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t}{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{t^{2}}+\frac{t^{2}}{t^{2}\cdot t_{i}^{2}}+\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{t^{2}(1-t)}+\frac{t^{2}(1-t)\log\mathcal{M}}{t^{2}(1-t)\cdot t_{i}^{2}(1-t_{i})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t}{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{t^{2}}+\frac{4}{t^{2}}+\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{t^{2}(1-t)}+\frac{4\log\mathcal{M}}{t^{2}(1-t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t}{2}\cdot\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{t^{2}(1-t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log\mathcal{M}}{T^{2}},

where we used
\frac{t_{i+1}}{t_{i}}\leq 2
, and the condition in Lemma
C.2
is satisfied. Therefore, by Lemma
C.2
and inequality
\mathrm{TV}(p_{T},\overline{p}_{T})\lesssim\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}(p_{T},\overline{p}_{T})}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}^{2}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{K-1}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}\frac{t}{2}\cdot\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}+2\left(\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right)\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right]\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{K-1}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}t\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right)\right].

We first bound the term
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)
. For convenience, we denote
\Delta:=\max_{0\leq i\leq K-1}(t_{i+1}-t_{i})
, then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{t}-\frac{1}{t_{i}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{1-t}^{F}-\mathbf{y}_{1-t_{i}}^{F}\right\|^{2}+\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{2}t_{i}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\left((t-t_{i})^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}\|^{2}+(\sqrt{1-t_{i}^{2}}-\sqrt{1-t^{2}})^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\mathbf{z}\|^{2}\right)+\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{2}t_{i}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\left((t-t_{i})^{2}+\left(\frac{t^{2}-t_{i}^{2}}{\sqrt{1-t_{i}^{2}}+\sqrt{1-t^{2}}}\right)^{2}\right)+\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{2}t_{i}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{2}t_{i}^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{2t(t-t_{i})}{2\sqrt{1-t^{2}}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Delta^{2}}{t^{4}}+\frac{\Delta^{2}}{1-t}.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{K-1}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}t\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{2}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{1}{t^{3}}\mathrm{d}t+\Delta^{2}\int_{T}^{1-T}\frac{t}{1-t}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}+\Delta^{2}\cdot\log\mathcal{M}.

Next, we bound the term
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)
. We can decompose this term into following four terms.

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}\right\|^{2}\right)}_{(\mathrm{I})}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})}{t}\right\|^{2}\right)}_{\mathrm{(II)}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)}_{(\mathrm{III})}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)}_{(\mathrm{IV})}.

We bound these four terms separately. Before we begin, let’ s give a bound for
\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|
which is similar to
\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|
. Again by Lemma
E.3
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{1-t_{i}}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}-m_{1-t_{i}})_{+}}{\sigma_{1-t_{i}}}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma_{1-t_{i}}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|m_{1-t}\mathbf{y}_{0}^{F}+\sigma_{1-t}\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}-m_{1-t_{i}})_{+}}{\sigma_{1-t_{i}}}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-t_{i}^{2}}}\cdot\left(\frac{(t-t_{i}+\sqrt{1-t^{2}}\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty})_{+}}{\sqrt{1-t_{i}^{2}}}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-t_{i}}}\cdot\left(\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{\sqrt{1-t_{i}}}+\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}\right)\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-t_{i}}}\cdot\left(\|\mathbf{z}\|_{\infty}+1+1\right),

where we used
t\geq t_{i}
and
t\leq 1
. Now we’ ve already derived

\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{1-t},\quad\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{1-t_{i}}.

We first bound term
(\mathrm{I})
, which corresponds to

\displaystyle\mathrm{(I)}=\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}.

For any
\epsilon>0
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle(\mathrm{I})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}.

According to Lagrange’ s theorem and Lemma
E.3
, there exists
t^{\prime}\in[t_{i},t]
such that

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|

\displaystyle=(t-t_{i})\cdot\|\partial_{t}\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|\Big|_{t=t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(t-t_{i})\cdot\left[\frac{|\partial_{t}\sigma_{1-t}|+|\partial_{t}m_{1-t}|}{\sigma_{1-t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}-m_{1-t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{1-t}^{2}}\vee 1\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\right]\Bigg|_{t=t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t-t_{i}}{(1-t^{\prime})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot\left[\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}-m_{1-t^{\prime}})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{1-t^{\prime}}^{2}}\vee 1\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\right].

Therefore, for term
(\mathrm{I})
we have

\displaystyle(\mathrm{I})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{2}(1-t^{\prime})^{3}}\cdot\left(\frac{1-t^{2}}{1-{t^{\prime}}^{2}}\right)^{3}\log^{3}\epsilon^{-1}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-t}+\frac{1}{1-t_{i}}\right)\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right).

By Lemma
E.4
, it holds
