\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right)\lesssim\sigma_{1-t}\epsilon\lesssim\epsilon.

Thus we continue to derive that

\displaystyle(\mathrm{I})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{2}(1-t^{\prime})^{3}}\cdot\log^{3}\epsilon^{-1}+\frac{\epsilon}{t^{2}(1-t)}

(27)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{2}(1-t^{\prime})^{3}}\cdot\log^{3}\epsilon^{-1}+\frac{\epsilon}{t^{2}(1-t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}\cdot\frac{\log^{3}\epsilon^{-1}+\epsilon}{t^{2}(1-t)},

where we used
t^{\prime}\leq t
.

Next, we bound term
(\mathrm{II})
, which expresses

(\mathrm{II})=\frac{1}{t^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2}.

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}},\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-t_{i}}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\frac{1}{1-t_{i}}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}},\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}.

By Lemma
E.4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right)\lesssim\sigma_{1-t}\epsilon\lesssim\epsilon,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right)\lesssim\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\epsilon\lesssim\epsilon.

Likewise, using Lagrange’ s theorem and Lemma
E.3
, there exists
\mathbf{y}^{\prime}
between
\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}
and
\mathbf{y}_{t}
such that

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}},\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}^{\prime}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}},\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1}}{\sigma_{1-t_{i}}^{4}}\cdot\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|^{2}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})-\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t}+C\sigma_{1-t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}},\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1}}{\sigma_{1-t_{i}}^{4}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1}}{(1-t_{i})^{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\sqrt{1-t_{i}^{2}}-\sqrt{1-t^{2}}\right)^{2}+(t-t_{i})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1}(t-t_{i})^{2}}{(1-t)^{3}}.

Therefore
(\mathrm{II})
is bounded by

(\mathrm{II})\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\left(\frac{\epsilon}{1-t_{i}}+\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}\cdot\frac{\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1}}{1-t}\right)\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{2}}\left(\frac{\epsilon}{1-t}+\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}\cdot\frac{\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1}}{1-t}\right).

(28)

Then, we bound the third term
(\mathrm{III})
. According to Lemma
E.3
, it holds that

\displaystyle(\mathrm{III})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{t}-\frac{1}{t_{i}}\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{4}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{4}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}\|_{\infty}>m_{1-t_{i}}+C\sigma_{1-t_{i}}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{4}}\cdot\left(\frac{\log\epsilon^{-1}}{1-t}+\frac{\epsilon}{1-t}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(t-t_{i})^{2}}{t^{4}(1-t)}\cdot(\log\epsilon^{-1}+\epsilon).

Thus, term
(\mathrm{III})
is bounded by

(\mathrm{III})\lesssim\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}\cdot\frac{\log\epsilon^{-1}+\epsilon}{t^{2}(1-t)}.

(29)

The last term
(\mathrm{IV})
is bounded by

\displaystyle(\mathrm{IV})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{t_{i}^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},y_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})\|^{2}

(30)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t\cdot t_{i}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},y_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})\|^{2}.

Combining (
27
)-(
30
)
we obtain

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{K-1}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}t\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}\left(\log^{3}\epsilon^{-1}+\epsilon\right)\log\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1}\cdot\log\mathcal{M}+\epsilon\log\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\frac{\Delta^{2}}{T^{2}}\left(\log\epsilon^{-1}+\epsilon\right)\log\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{K-1}\frac{t_{i+1}-t_{i}}{t_{i}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\|\nabla\log p_{1-t_{i}}(\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}|\mathbf{x})-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\right).

By taking
\epsilon=n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}
,
\mathcal{M}=\lfloor n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\rfloor+1\lesssim n^{\frac{1}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}
,
C_{T}=2\beta
,
T=\mathcal{M}^{-C_{T}}
,
\Delta=\max_{0\leq i\leq K-1}(t_{i+1}-t_{i})=\mathcal{O}\left(\min\left\{\Delta_{n},n^{-\frac{3\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\right\}\right)
and using (
12
), we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{K-1}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}t\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t}}{t}-\frac{\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}}}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}},

and

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\sum_{i=0}^{K-1}\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{i+1}}t\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\frac{\nabla\log p_{1-t}(\mathbf{y}_{t}|\mathbf{x})}{t}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}(1-t_{i},\mathbf{y}_{t_{i}},\mathbf{x})}{t_{i}}\right\|^{2}\mathrm{d}t\right)\right]\lesssim n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{19}n,

for sufficiently large
n
, which implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}^{2}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

(31)

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\frac{\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{\frac{19}{2}}n.

Combining this with (
25
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\overline{p}_{T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathrm{TV}(p_{1-T}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{I}_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\frac{\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{\frac{19}{2}}n+n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\frac{\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{\frac{19}{2}}n.

The proof is complete.
∎

Based on Theorem
3.4
and Lemma
3.5
, we can bound
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]
.

Proof of Theorem
3.6
.

Combining (
24
) and (
31
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}[\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))]

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\frac{\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{\frac{19}{2}}n.

The proof is complete.
∎

Appendix D
Proofs of Bootstrap Convergence

Proof of Theorem
3.7
.

It follows that

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{R}(\mathbf{x})-\widehat{R}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\widehat{f}(\mathbf{x})-f_{0}(\mathbf{x})-\widehat{f}^{*}(\mathbf{x})+\widehat{f}(\mathbf{x})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\widehat{f}(\mathbf{x})-f_{0}(\mathbf{x})\right|+\left|\widehat{f}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-\widehat{f}(\mathbf{x})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(j)}-f_{0}(\mathbf{x})\right|+\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\widehat{Y}^{*,(j)}_{\mathbf{x}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(j)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(j)}-f_{0}(\mathbf{x})\right|+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\widehat{Y}^{*,(j)}_{\mathbf{x}}-\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(j)}\right|.

(32)

We further analyze the two terms on the
right-hand side of (
D
), which yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(j)}-f_{0}(\mathbf{x})\right|\bigg|\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(1)}-f_{0}(\mathbf{x})\right|\bigg|\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(1)}-Y_{\mathbf{x}}\right|\bigg|\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(1)}-Y_{\mathbf{x}}\right|^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathbf{x}\right]\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(33)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\widehat{Y}^{*,(j)}_{\mathbf{x}}-\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(j)}\right|\bigg|\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{D}^{*},\mathcal{T}^{*},\mathcal{Z}^{*},\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{Y}^{*,(1)}_{\mathbf{x}}-\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(1)}\right|\Big|\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{D}^{*},\mathcal{T}^{*},\mathcal{Z}^{*},\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{Y}^{*,(1)}_{\mathbf{x}}-\widehat{Y}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(1)}\right|^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{D}^{*},\mathcal{T}^{*},\mathcal{Z}^{*},\mathbf{x}\right]\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(34)

The above inequalities (
D
)-(
D
) imply that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{D}^{*},\mathcal{T}^{*},\mathcal{Z}^{*}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\mathcal{W}_{1}\left(\widehat{R}(\mathbf{x}),\widehat{R}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{0}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))\right]

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{D}^{*},\mathcal{T}^{*},\mathcal{Z}^{*}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{p}_{T}^{B}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}),p_{T}^{B,*}(\cdot|\mathbf{x}))\right].

By Theorem
3.6
, we thus have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{D}^{*},\mathcal{T}^{*},\mathcal{Z}^{*}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{x}}\left[\mathcal{W}_{1}\left(\widehat{R}(\mathbf{x}),\widehat{R}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\frac{\beta}{d_{\mathcal{X}}+d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2\beta}}\log^{\frac{19}{2}}n.

The proof is complete.
∎

Proof of Theorem
3.8
.

Denote

H(r):=\mathbb{P}(\widehat{R}(\mathbf{x})\leq r),\penalty\ \widehat{H}(r):=\mathbb{P}(\widehat{R}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\leq r).

From Theorem
3.7
, we can deduce that

\widehat{H}
converges to
H
.
Furthermore, since
\widehat{H}^{*}
converges to
\widehat{H}
, it follows that

\widehat{H}^{*}
converges to
H
as well.
Conversely, the consistency of their quantiles is preserved, implying that the inverse functions
(\widehat{H}^{*})^{-1}
converge to
H^{-1}
.
This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix E
Auxiliary Lemmas

E.1
Several High-Probability Bounds

Following
Oko et al. 2023
,
we provide several high-probability bounds in this section. In the following,
we denote
m_{t}:=1-t
and
\sigma_{t}:=\sqrt{t(2-t)}
.

E.1.1
Bounds on
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

In this section, we give the upper and lower bounds
on
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
.

Lemma E.1
.

For any
\mathbf{x}\in[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
, the following upper and lower bounds on
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
hold:

\exp\left(-\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\lesssim p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\lesssim\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

(35)

Proof.

This proof can be divided into two cases:
\mathbf{y}\in[-m_{t},m_{t}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
and
\mathbf{y}\notin[-m_{t},m_{t}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
.

Case I (
\mathbf{y}\in[-m_{t},m_{t}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
):

Given that
p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\leq C_{u}
,
we have

\displaystyle p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{u}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{\mathbf{I}_{\{\mathbf{y}_{1}\in[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\}}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{u}2^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}.

Additionally, we also have

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq C_{u}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\leq\frac{C_{u}}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}.

Thus,
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
is bounded by
\min\left\{\frac{C_{u}2^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}},\frac{C_{u}}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\right\}=\mathcal{O}(1)
.

The lower bound can be derived as follows:

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\geq C_{l}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{\mathbf{I}_{\{\mathbf{y}_{1}\in[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\}}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}.

Let
\mathbf{z}=\frac{\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\sigma_{t}}
, then we have

\displaystyle p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle\geq C_{l}\cdot\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{\mathbf{I}_{\left\{\mathbf{z}\in\left[\frac{\mathbf{y}-m_{t}}{\sigma_{t}},\frac{\mathbf{y}+m_{t}}{\sigma_{t}}\right]\right\}}}{(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{z}\|^{2}}{2}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{z}

\displaystyle\geq C_{l}\cdot\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{\mathbf{I}_{\left\{\mathbf{z}\in[\mathbf{y}-m_{t},\mathbf{y}+m_{t}]\right\}}}{(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{z}\|^{2}}{2}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{z}

\displaystyle\geq C_{l}\cdot\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{(2m_{t})^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}{(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-2d_{\mathcal{Y}}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim 1.

Case II (
\mathbf{y}\notin[-m_{t},m_{t}]^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
):

Let
r=\frac{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t}}{\sigma_{t}}
and
|y_{i^{*}}|=\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}
. For
i\neq i^{*}
, if
y_{i}>m_{t}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\mathbf{I}_{\{y_{1,i}\in[-1,1]\}}}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{-1}^{1}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(m_{t}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m_{t}}\cdot\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{y_{1,i}^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}\leq\frac{1}{2m_{t}}.

If
y_{i}<-m_{t}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\mathbf{I}_{\{y_{1,i}\in[-1,1]\}}}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{-1}^{1}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(-m_{t}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m_{t}}\cdot\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{y_{1,i}^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}\leq\frac{1}{2m_{t}}.

Also, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\mathbf{I}_{\{y_{1,i}\in[-1,1]\}}}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}.

Therefore, we have

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}-1}}\int_{-1}^{1}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i^{*}}-m_{t}y_{1,i^{*}})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i^{*}},

and

\displaystyle p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}-1}}\int_{-1}^{1}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i^{*}}-m_{t}y_{1,i^{*}})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i^{*}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

Let
z_{i^{*}}=\frac{y_{i^{*}}-m_{t}y_{1,i^{*}}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}
, then

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\cdot\int_{\frac{y_{i^{*}}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{\frac{y_{i^{*}}+m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i^{*}}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i^{*}}.

If
y_{i^{*}}>m_{t}
, then

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\cdot\int_{\frac{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i^{*}}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i^{*}}\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

If
y_{i}^{*}<-m_{t}
,
y_{i}^{*}=-\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}
, then

\displaystyle p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\cdot\int_{-\frac{y_{i^{*}}+m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{-\frac{y_{i^{*}}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i^{*}}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i^{*}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\cdot\int_{\frac{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i^{*}}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i^{*}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

Therefore,
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
is bounded by

\displaystyle p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\left\{\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}},\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\right\}\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

On the other hand, for
1\leq i\leq d_{\mathcal{Y}}
, let
z_{i}=\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}
, then we define

\displaystyle g(y_{i})

\displaystyle:=\int_{-1}^{1}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}(2\pi)^{1/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m_{t}}\int_{\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{\frac{y_{i}+m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i}.

If
y_{i}>m_{t}
, then
g(y_{i})
is a decreasing function. Therefore,

\displaystyle g(y_{i})\geq g(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m_{t}}\int_{\frac{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{\frac{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}+m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{m_{t}}\int_{\frac{r}{\sqrt{2}}}^{\frac{r}{\sqrt{2}}+\sqrt{2}m_{t}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\exp(-r^{2}-4m_{t}^{2})

\displaystyle\gtrsim\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right),

where we used equality
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
and
\exp(-4m_{t}^{2})\geq\exp(-4)
.
If
y_{i}<-m_{t}
, then

g(y_{i})=\frac{1}{m_{t}}\int_{\frac{-y_{i}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{\frac{-y_{i}+m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i}.

It is easy to check that
g(y_{i})
is a increasing function. Therefore,

\displaystyle g(y_{i})\geq g(-\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m_{t}}\int_{\frac{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}^{\frac{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}+m_{t}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{t}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\exp(-z_{i}^{2})\mathrm{d}z_{i}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

The above discussion implies that

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\gtrsim\exp\left(-\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

Based on the above discussion of the two cases,
we finally obtain

\exp\left(-\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\lesssim p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\lesssim\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

The proof is complete.
∎

E.1.2
Bounds on the derivatives of
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
and
\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})

In this section, we give the upper bounds on the derivatives of
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
and
\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
. We first state the following lemma.

Lemma E.2

(Integral Clipping)
.

Let
\mathbf{x}\in[-1,1]^{d_{\mathcal{X}}}
,
\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
and
\boldsymbol{\alpha}\in\mathbb{N}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
. For any
0<\epsilon<e^{-1}
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle-\int_{A_{\mathbf{y}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\Bigg|\lesssim\epsilon,

where
A_{\mathbf{y}}=\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}a_{i,\mathbf{y}}
with
a_{i,\mathbf{y}}=\bigg[\frac{y_{i}-C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}{m_{t}},\frac{y_{i}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}{m_{t}}\bigg].

Proof.

It follows that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\backslash A_{\mathbf{y}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{u}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\backslash A_{\mathbf{y}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{i,1}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\mathbf{I}_{\{\|\mathbf{y}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{u}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\cdots\times\mathbb{R}\times(\mathbb{R}\backslash a_{j,\mathbf{y}})\times\mathbb{R}\times\cdots\times\mathbb{R}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,i}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{u}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\Bigg[\left(\prod_{i=1,i\neq j}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,i}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}\right)

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}\backslash a_{j,\mathbf{y}}}\left|\frac{y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{j}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,j}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,j}\Bigg].

For
i\neq j
, let
z_{i}=\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}
.
Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,i}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{t}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|z_{i}|^{\alpha_{i}}\exp\left(-\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{t}}.

Additionally, since for any
\alpha>0
,
|z|^{\alpha}\exp(-z^{2}/2)=\mathcal{O}(1)
, it also have

\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,i}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}.

Thus, we obtain

\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,i}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,i}\lesssim\min\left\{\frac{1}{m_{t}},\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\right\}=\mathcal{O}(1).

For
i=j
, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}\backslash a_{j,\mathbf{y}}}\left|\frac{y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{j}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,j}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,j}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{t}}\cdot\int_{|z_{j}|\geq C\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}|z_{j}|^{\alpha_{j}}\exp\left(-\frac{z_{j}^{2}}{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z_{j}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{t}}\cdot 2\int_{z_{j}\geq C\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}z_{j}^{\alpha_{j}}\exp\left(-\frac{z_{j}^{2}}{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z_{j}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{t}}\cdot\epsilon^{\frac{C^{2}}{2}}(C^{2}\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{\alpha_{j}}{2}}.

Since for any
\alpha>0
,
z^{\alpha}\exp(-z^{2}/2)
is decreasing for
|z|>\sqrt{\alpha}
, we can choose
C\geq\sqrt{\alpha}
and
\epsilon<e^{-1}
, then it holds that

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}\backslash a_{j,\mathbf{y}}}\left|\frac{y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{j}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,j}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,j}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\epsilon^{\frac{C^{2}}{2}}(C^{2}\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{\alpha_{j}}{2}}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}\backslash a_{j,\mathbf{y}}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,j}|\leq 1\}}\mathrm{d}y_{1,j}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\epsilon^{\frac{C^{2}}{2}}(C^{2}\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{\alpha_{j}}{2}}\cdot 2^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\epsilon^{\frac{C^{2}}{2}}(C^{2}\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{\alpha_{j}}{2}}\cdot

Taking
C\geq\max\{2,\max_{1\leq i\leq d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\sqrt{\alpha_{i}}\}
, we obtain

\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}\backslash a_{j,\mathbf{y}}}\left|\frac{y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{j}}\mathbf{I}_{\{|y_{1,j}|\leq 1\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{j}-m_{t}y_{1,j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}y_{1,j}\lesssim\epsilon\cdot\min\left\{\frac{1}{m_{t}},\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\right\}\lesssim\epsilon,

which implies that

\Bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\backslash A_{\mathbf{y}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\Bigg|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\epsilon\lesssim\epsilon.

The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma E.3

(Boundedness of Derivatives)
.

For any
k\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
and
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
, the following upper bounds hold:

|\partial_{y_{i_{1}}}\cdots\partial_{y_{i_{k}}}\partial_{x_{j_{1}}}\cdots\partial_{x_{j_{\ell}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{k}}

(36)

where
i_{1},\cdots i_{k}\in\{1,\cdots,d_{\mathcal{Y}}\}
,
j_{1},\cdots,j_{\ell}\in\{1,\cdots,d_{\mathcal{X}}\}
. Specially, when
\ell=0
, (
36
) reduces to

|\partial_{y_{i_{1}}}\partial_{y_{i_{2}}}\cdots\partial_{y_{i_{k}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{k}}.

Moreover, we have

\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}}{\sigma_{t}}\vee 1\right),

(37)

\|\partial_{y_{i}}\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\vee 1\right),\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ 1\leq i\leq d_{\mathcal{Y}},

(38)

and

\|\partial_{t}\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}|+|\partial_{t}m_{t}|}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\vee 1\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}.

(39)

Proof.

We first prove (
36
). Let
f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})=p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
. For multi-indices
\boldsymbol{\alpha}\in\mathbb{N}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}\in\mathbb{N}^{d_{\mathcal{X}}}
, we denote
f_{1}^{(\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\beta})}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}):=\partial_{y_{1}}^{\alpha_{1}}\cdots\partial_{y_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}^{\alpha_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\partial_{x_{1}}^{\beta_{1}}\cdots\partial_{x_{d_{\mathcal{X}}}}^{\beta_{d_{\mathcal{X}}}}f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
. We define
B_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}:=\{\mathbf{u}\in\mathbb{N}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}|u_{i}\leq\alpha_{i}\penalty\ (i=1,2,\cdots,d_{\mathcal{Y}})\}
. Then, it holds that

\partial_{y_{1}}^{\alpha_{1}}\partial_{y_{2}}^{\alpha_{2}}\cdots\partial_{y_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}^{\alpha_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}e^{-\|\mathbf{y}\|^{2}/2}=\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in B_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}}C_{\mathbf{u}}\partial_{y_{1}}^{u_{1}}\partial_{y_{2}}^{u_{2}}\cdots\partial_{y_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}^{u_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}e^{-\|\mathbf{y}\|^{2}/2}

with some constants
C_{\mathbf{u}}
. Then, we can write
f_{1}^{(\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\beta})}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
as

f_{1}^{(\boldsymbol{\alpha},\boldsymbol{\beta})}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})=\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\alpha_{i}}}\cdot\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in B_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}}C_{\mathbf{u}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{u_{i}}\frac{\partial_{\mathbf{x}}^{\boldsymbol{\beta}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}.

Taking
\alpha_{i_{1}},\cdots,\alpha_{i_{k}}=1
,
\beta_{j_{1}},\cdots,\beta_{j_{\ell}}=1
, and the others as 0,
since

|\partial_{\mathbf{x}}^{\boldsymbol{\beta}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})|\leq C_{\mathcal{X}}
, then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{u}\in B_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}}C_{\mathbf{u}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{u_{i}}\frac{|\partial_{\mathbf{x}}^{\boldsymbol{\beta}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})|}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\left\{\frac{1}{m_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}},\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1),

which implies that

|\partial_{y_{i_{1}}}\cdots\partial_{y_{i_{k}}}\partial_{x_{j_{1}}}\cdots\partial_{x_{j_{\ell}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{k}}.

Specially, when
\ell=0
, i.e.,
\boldsymbol{\beta}=\boldsymbol{0}
, it holds that

|\partial_{y_{i_{1}}}\partial_{y_{i_{2}}}\cdots\partial_{y_{i_{k}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{k}}.

Next, we prove (
37
) and (
38
). For convenience, we focus on the first coordinate of
\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
, and all other coordinates of
\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})
are bounded in the same manner. Let
f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}):=[\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{1}
.
Then, we have

[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{1}=\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})},\penalty\ [\partial_{y_{i}}\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{1}=\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\left(\frac{\partial_{y_{i}}f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}-\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})(\partial_{y_{i}}f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x}))}{f_{1}^{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\right).

Moreover,

\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}=\frac{-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\left(\frac{y_{1}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}},

\frac{\partial_{y_{i}}f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}=\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}},

\frac{\partial_{y_{i}}f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}=-\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{\{i=1\}}-\frac{y_{1}-m_{t}y_{1,1}}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}.

The integral terms in the three equations above can be expressed in the following unified form:

\frac{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}

(40)

with
\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\alpha_{i}\leq 2
.
According to Lemma
E.2
, for any
0<\epsilon<e^{-1}
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle-\int_{A_{\mathbf{y}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left(\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}\Bigg|\lesssim\epsilon,

where
A_{\mathbf{y}}=\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}a_{i,\mathbf{y}}
with
a_{i,\mathbf{y}}=\bigg[\frac{y_{i}-C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}{m_{t}},\frac{y_{i}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}{m_{t}}\bigg].
Therefore, when
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\gtrsim\epsilon
, we have

\displaystyle|\eqref{eq: eq_unified}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\int_{A_{\mathbf{y}}}\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int_{A_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}+\mathcal{O}(1)

(41)

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{\mathbf{y}_{1}\in A_{\mathbf{y}}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\left|\frac{y_{i}-m_{t}y_{1,i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right|^{\alpha_{i}}\right]+\mathcal{O}(1)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(C^{2}\log\epsilon^{-1}\right)^{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\alpha_{i}}{2}}+\mathcal{O}(1)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\log\epsilon^{-1}\right)^{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\alpha_{i}}{2}},

which implies that

\left|\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}},\penalty\ \left|\frac{\partial_{y_{i}}f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\right|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}{\sigma_{t}},\penalty\ \left|\frac{\partial_{y_{i}}f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\right|\lesssim\frac{\log\epsilon^{-1}}{\sigma_{t}}.

Then, we obtain

\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}{\sigma_{t}},\penalty\ \|\partial_{y_{i}}\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{\log\epsilon^{-1}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}.

By Lemma
E.1
,
p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\gtrsim\exp\left(-\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)
.
Replacing
\epsilon
with
\exp\left(-\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)
, we have

\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}}{\sigma_{t}}\vee 1\right)

and

\|\partial_{y_{i}}\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\vee 1\right),\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ 1\leq i\leq d_{\mathcal{Y}}.

Finally, we prove (
39
). By a simple calculation, we have

\partial_{t}[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{1}=\left(\partial_{t}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{t}f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\cdot\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{t}f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}.

We calculate these three terms separately.

\left(\partial_{t}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}=-\frac{\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot[\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})]_{1},

\frac{\partial_{t}f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}=\frac{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}\left(\sigma_{t}^{-3}\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}-d_{\mathcal{Y}}\sigma_{t}^{-1}\right)-\sigma_{t}^{-2}\langle\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}\rangle\partial_{t}m_{t}}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{t}f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}\cdot\frac{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\frac{(y_{1}-m_{t}y_{1,1})}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\left(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{Y}}}{\sigma_{t}}-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{3}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\partial_{t}m_{t}\cdot\frac{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{y_{1,1}}{\sigma_{t}}-\frac{y_{1}-m_{t}y_{1,1}}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{\langle\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}\rangle}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}{\int\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}(2\pi)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}/2}}p_{0}(\mathbf{y}_{1}|\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\frac{\|\mathbf{y}-m_{t}\mathbf{y}_{1}\|}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}_{1}}

According to (
41
), we have

\left|\left(\partial_{t}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\right|\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}|}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}}{\sigma_{t}}\vee 1\right),

\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{t}f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\cdot\frac{f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\right|\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}|+|\partial_{t}m_{t}|}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\vee 1\right)^{\frac{3}{2}},

\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\cdot\frac{\partial_{t}f_{2}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}{f_{1}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})}\right|\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}|+|\partial_{t}m_{t}|}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\vee 1\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Combining the above three inequalities, we finally obtain

\|\partial_{t}\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{t}\sigma_{t}|+|\partial_{t}m_{t}|}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})_{+}^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\vee 1\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma E.4

(Error Bounds for Clipping)
.

Let
0<\epsilon,\epsilon_{1}<1
. For all
0<t\leq 1
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\frac{\epsilon}{\sigma_{t}},

(42)

\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\sigma_{t}\epsilon.

(43)

Moreover, for
\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}
, it holds that

\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq\epsilon_{1}\}}\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}(\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2}{2}},

(44)

\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq\epsilon_{1}\}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}(\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}{2}}.

(45)

Proof.

We first prove (
42
). For
\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\geq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}
, according to Lemma
E.1
and Lemma
E.3
, we have

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\frac{(\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}-m_{t})^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}.

Without loss of generality, we set
|y_{1}|=\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{|y_{1}|>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}\int_{|y_{2}|\leq|y_{1}|}\cdots\int_{|y_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}|\leq|y_{1}|}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(|y_{1}|-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\frac{(|y_{1}|-m_{t})^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\mathrm{d}y_{2}\cdots\mathrm{d}y_{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\mathrm{d}y_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{|y_{1}|>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(|y_{1}|-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\frac{(|y_{1}|-m_{t})^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot|y_{1}|^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}-1}\mathrm{d}y_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{y_{1}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y_{1}-m_{t})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)\frac{(y_{1}-m_{t})^{2}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}\cdot y_{1}^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}-1}\mathrm{d}y_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{C\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\exp\left(-\frac{r^{2}}{2}\right)r^{2}(m_{t}+r\sigma_{t})^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}-1}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{C\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\exp\left(-\frac{r^{2}}{2}\right)r^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+1}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{t}}\epsilon^{\frac{C^{2}}{2}}(\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+1}{2}},

where we let
r=\frac{y_{1}-m_{t}}{\sigma_{t}}
. Taking
C\geq 2
and use
\epsilon(\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+1}{2}}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, it holds that

\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}\lesssim\frac{\epsilon}{\sigma_{t}}.

Additionally,
(
43
) can be derived in the same manner, that is,

\displaystyle\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}>m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{C\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}^{\infty}\sigma_{t}\exp\left(-\frac{r^{2}}{2}\right)\cdot r^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}-1}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{t}\epsilon.

Now, we consider the second part of this lemma. For
\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}
, by Lemma
E.1
and Lemma
E.3
, we have

p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq\epsilon_{1}\}}\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\cdot\frac{\log\epsilon^{-1}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq\epsilon_{1}\}}\|\nabla\log p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\|^{2}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\cdot\frac{\epsilon_{1}\log\epsilon^{-1}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\sigma_{t}^{2}}(\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}+2}{2}}.

In the same manner, we also have

\displaystyle\int_{\|\mathbf{y}\|_{\infty}\leq m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}}p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\mathbf{I}_{\{p_{t}(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})\leq\epsilon\}}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(m_{t}+C\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log\epsilon^{-1}}\right)^{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}\cdot\epsilon_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{1}(\log\epsilon^{-1})^{\frac{d_{\mathcal{Y}}}{2}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

E.2
Auxiliary Lemmas on ReLU Network Approximation

In this section, we summarize existing results and fundamental tools for function approximation using neural networks. See
Oko et al. 2023
;
Fu et al. 2024
for more details.

E.2.1
Construction of a large ReLU network

Lemma E.5

(Neural Network Concatenation)
.

For a series of ReLU networks
\mathbf{b}_{1}:\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}
,
\mathbf{b}_{2}:\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{3}}
,
\cdots
,
\mathbf{b}_{k}:\mathbb{R}^{d_{k}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{k+1}}
with
\mathbf{b}_{i}\in\mathrm{NN}(L_{i},M_{i},J_{i},\kappa_{i})\penalty\ (i=1,2,\cdots,k)
, there exists a neural network
\mathbf{b}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
satisfying
\mathbf{b}(\mathbf{x})=\mathbf{b}_{k}\circ\mathbf{b}_{k-1}\circ\cdots\circ\mathbf{b}_{1}(\mathbf{x})
for all
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}
, with

L=\sum_{i=1}^{k}L_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ M\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{k}M_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ J\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{k}J_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ \kappa\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\kappa_{i}.

Lemma E.6

(Identity Function)
.

Given
d\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
L\geq 2
, there exists a neural network
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{Id},L}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
that realizes
d
-dimensional identity function
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{Id},L}(\mathbf{x})=\mathbf{x}
,
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Here

M=2d,\penalty\ \penalty\ J=2dL,\penalty\ \penalty\ \kappa=1.

Lemma E.7

(Neural Network Parallelization)
.

For a series of ReLU networks
\mathbf{b}_{i}:\mathbb{R}^{d_{i}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{i}^{\prime}}
with
\mathbf{b}_{i}\in\mathrm{NN}(L_{i},M_{i},J_{i},\kappa_{i})\penalty\ (i=1,2,\cdots,k)
, there exists a neural network
\mathbf{b}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
satisfying
\mathbf{b}(\mathbf{x})=[\mathbf{b}_{1}^{\top}(\mathbf{x}_{1}),\mathbf{b}_{2}^{\top}(\mathbf{x}_{2}),\cdots,\mathbf{b}_{k}^{\top}(\mathbf{x}_{k})]:\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}+d_{2}+\cdots+d_{k}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}^{\prime}+d_{2}^{\prime}+\cdots+d_{k}^{\prime}}
for all
\mathbf{x}=(\mathbf{x}_{1}^{\top},\mathbf{x}_{2}^{\top},\cdots,\mathbf{x}_{k}^{\top})^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}+d_{2}+\cdots+d_{k}}
(
\mathbf{x}_{i}
can be shared), with

\displaystyle L=L,\penalty\ \penalty\ M\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{k}M_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ J\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{k}J_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ \kappa\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\kappa_{i}\penalty\ \penalty\ (\text{when}\penalty\ L=L_{i}\penalty\ \text{holds for all}\penalty\ i),

\displaystyle L=\max_{1\leq i\leq k}L_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ M\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{k}M_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ J\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{k}(J_{i}+Ld_{i}^{\prime}),\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ \kappa\leq\max{\{\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\kappa_{i},1\}}\penalty\ (\text{otherwise}).

Moreover, for
\mathbf{x}_{1}=\mathbf{x}_{2}=\cdots=\mathbf{x}_{k}=\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
d_{1}^{\prime}=d_{2}^{\prime}=\cdots=d_{k}^{\prime}=d^{\prime}
, there exists a neural network
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{sum}}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
that realizes
\mathbf{b}_{\mathrm{sum}}(\mathbf{x})=\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathbf{b}_{i}(\mathbf{x})
with

L=\max_{1\leq i\leq k}L_{i}+1,\penalty\ \penalty\ M\leq 4\sum_{i=1}^{k}M_{i},\penalty\ \penalty\ J\leq 4\sum_{i=1}^{k}(J_{i}+Ld_{i}^{\prime})+2M,\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ \kappa\leq\max{\{\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\kappa_{i},1\}}.

E.2.2
Approximation of basic functions with ReLU network

Lemma E.8

(Approximating the Multiple Products)
.

Let
d\geq 2
,
C\geq 1
. For any
\epsilon>0
, there exists a neural network
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{prod}}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
with
L=\mathcal{O}(\log d(\log d+\log\epsilon^{-1}+d\log C))
,
M=48d
,
J=\mathcal{O}(d(\log d+\log\epsilon^{-1}+d\log C)
,
\kappa=C^{d}
such that

\left|\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{prod}}(x_{1}^{\prime},x_{2}^{\prime},\cdots,x_{d}^{\prime})-\prod_{i=1}^{d}x_{i}\right|\leq\epsilon+dC^{d-1}\epsilon_{0},

for all
\mathbf{x}\in[-C,C]^{d}
and
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{\infty}\leq\epsilon_{0}
. Moreover,
|\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{prod}}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime})|\leq C^{d}
for all
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{prod}}(x_{1}^{\prime},x_{2}^{\prime},\cdots,x_{d}^{\prime})=0
if at least one of
x_{i}^{\prime}
is 0.

Remark E.1
.

We note that some of
x_{i}
,
x_{j}

(i\neq j)
can be shared. For
\prod_{i=1}^{I}x_{i}^{u_{i}}
with
u_{i}\in\mathbb{N}_{+}(i=1,2,\cdots,I)
and
\sum_{i=1}^{I}u_{i}=d
, there exists a neural network satisfying the same bounds as above.

Lemma E.9

(Approximating the Reciprocal Function)
.

For any
0<\epsilon<1
, there exists a neural network
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{rec}}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
with
L=\mathcal{O}(\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1})
,
M=\mathcal{O}(\log^{3}\epsilon^{-1})
,
J=\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\epsilon^{-1})
and
\kappa=\mathcal{O}(\epsilon^{-2})
such that

\left|\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{rec}}(x^{\prime})-\frac{1}{x}\right|\leq\epsilon+\frac{|x^{\prime}-x|}{\epsilon^{2}},

for all
x\in[\epsilon,\epsilon^{-1}]
and
x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}
.

Lemma E.10

(Approximating the Square Root Function)
.

For any
0<\epsilon<1
, there exists a neural network
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{root}}\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
with
L=\mathcal{O}(\log^{2}\epsilon^{-1})
,
M=\mathcal{O}(\log^{3}\epsilon^{-1})
,
J=\mathcal{O}(\log^{4}\epsilon^{-1})
and
\kappa=\mathcal{O}(\epsilon^{-1})
such that

|\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{root}}(x^{\prime})-\sqrt{x}|\leq\epsilon+\frac{x^{\prime}-x}{\sqrt{\epsilon}},

for all
x\in[\epsilon,\epsilon^{-1}]
and
x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}
.

E.2.3
Clipping and switching functions

Lemma E.11

(Clipping Function)
.

For any
\mathbf{a}
,
\mathbf{b}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
a_{i}\leq b_{i}

(i=1,2,\cdots,d)
, there exists a clipping function
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{clip}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b})\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
with

L=2,\penalty\ M=2d,\penalty\ J=7d,\penalty\ \kappa=\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq d}\max\{|a_{i}|,|b_{i}|\}\right)\vee 1,

such that

\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{clip}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b})_{i}=\min\{b_{i},\max\{x_{i},a_{i}\}\}\penalty\ \penalty\ (i=1,2,\cdots,d).

When
a_{i}=c_{min}
and
b_{i}=c_{max}
for all
i
,
we sometimes denote
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{clip}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b})
as
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{clip}}(\mathbf{x},c_{min},c_{max})
using scalar values
c_{min}
and
c_{max}
.

Lemma E.12

(Switching Function)
.

Let
t_{1}<t_{2}<s_{1}<s_{2}
, and
f(t,\mathbf{x})
be a scalar-valued function (for a vector-valued function, we just apply this coordinate-wise). Assume that
|\phi_{1}(t,\mathbf{x})-f(t,\mathbf{x})|\leq\epsilon
on
[t_{1},s_{1}]
and
|\phi_{2}(t,\mathbf{x})-f(t,\mathbf{x})|\leq\epsilon
on
[t_{2},s_{2}]
. Then, there exist two neural networks
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1}(t,t_{2},s_{1})
and
\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\in\mathrm{NN}(L,M,J,\kappa)
with

L=3,\penalty\ \penalty\ M=2,\penalty\ \penalty\ S=8,\penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ \kappa=\max\{s_{1},(s_{1}-t_{2})^{-1}\}

such that

|\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\phi_{1}(t,\mathbf{x})+\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\phi_{2}(t,\mathbf{x})-f(t,\mathbf{x})|\leq\epsilon

holds for any
t\in[t_{1},s_{2}]
, where

\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},1}(t,t_{2},s_{1})=\frac{1}{s_{1}-t_{2}}\mathrm{ReLU}\left(s_{1}-\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{clip}}(t,t_{2},s_{1})\right),

\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{switch},2}(t,t_{2},s_{1})=\frac{1}{s_{1}-t_{2}}\mathrm{ReLU}\left(\mathrm{b}_{\mathrm{clip}}(t,t_{2},s_{1})-t_{2}\right).
